# Daily video prompt pack — 06/11/2026 · Monkey day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក)

Lucky signs: Monkey, Rat, Dragon · Careful: Tiger
Day element: Yang Wood 🌿 (Jia-shen day; ធាតុឈើ) · every action scene today carries Wood magic: fresh green leaves, glowing jade vines and young bamboo shoots that sprout and spiral through the air.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Human moments: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the golden magic opens a short vision of everyday Cambodian life with real feelings on real faces (the people never talk), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 06/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Monkey's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។

## Luckiest animal today – Monkey (វក) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color blue (ខៀវ) · best time 07:00–09:00 · lucky numbers 9 · 90 · 449 · 9944 · 96505 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey), the Monkey of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity and joyful energy: a playful, clever and cheerful expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands in a courtyard of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, beside two carved stone lions and a row of glowing oil lamps, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the monkey, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Monkey, in 80 seconds. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Monkey, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

1. Identity

★ 3. Question

5. Fact
   English: Today is a Monkey day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Monkey today's luckiest sign.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Monkey, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Monkey as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Monkey's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Monkey and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Monkey? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវកមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Monkey, people may describe you as clever, curious, and witty. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ឆ្លាតវៃ ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: clever, curious, and witty.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ឆ្លាតវៃ ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។
5. Symbol

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Monkey? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Friday, 6 November 2026, is a Monkey day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Monkey. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are clever, curious, and witty. Monkeys are quick learners who enjoy solving problems creatively.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Monkey? Your luck is strong today. Stay playful, clever and cheerful. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is blue. Wear something blue, or carry a small blue item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 7 to 9 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southeast. When you start something new, face the southeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 9, 90, 449!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Monkey's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Monkey can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: blue. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

7
00:00:18,016 --> 00:00:19,950

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,512
ស្វា ចូលចិត្តលេងសើច និងឆ្លាតវៃ។

9
00:00:22,562 --> 00:00:24,165

10
00:00:24,215 --> 00:00:26,562

11
00:00:26,612 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,629
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ជាមនុស្សឆ្លាតវៃ

13
00:00:32,679 --> 00:00:34,771
ចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងកំប្លែង។

14
00:00:34,821 --> 00:00:35,843
វកសិក្សារហ័ស

15
00:00:35,893 --> 00:00:39,950
និងចូលចិត្តដោះស្រាយបញ្ហាប្រកបដោយភាពច្នៃប្រឌិត។

16

សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ

17

18

សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19

20

21

22
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25
00:01:05,556 --> 00:01:08,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

26

និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

27

28
00:01:11,500 --> 00:01:14,750

29
00:01:14,800 --> 00:01:17,450
និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។

30

សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។

31

ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ខៀវ។

32
00:01:22,973 --> 00:01:26,076
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌ខៀវ ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ខៀវមួយ

33
00:01:26,126 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34

ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

35
00:01:32,407 --> 00:01:34,672

36

37

ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

38

39

41

ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

42

43
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

44

45

46
00:02:03,208 --> 00:02:05,327
គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ។

47
00:02:05,377 --> 00:02:07,120

48

49

សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក

50
00:02:12,542 --> 00:02:16,052
ជួនកាលលេងកំប្លែងពេក ឬពិបាកផ្តោតលើរឿងតែមួយ។

51

ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

52
00:02:18,136 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

53
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

54
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

55
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

56
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

57
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

58
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

59
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

60
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

61
00:02:41,680 --> 00:02:42,990
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

62
00:02:43,040 --> 00:02:45,470
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

63
00:02:45,520 --> 00:02:47,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

64
00:02:47,280 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 90 និងលេខ 449។

65
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

66
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

67
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

68
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

69
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-06-180s.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,743
Born in the Year of the Monkey?

2
00:00:02,793 --> 00:00:04,815
Here's your lucky color

3
00:00:04,865 --> 00:00:07,698
best time, and numbers for today

4
00:00:07,748 --> 00:00:09,950
plus one gentle reminder.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,206
Today, Friday, 6 November 2026

6
00:00:12,256 --> 00:00:13,409
is a Monkey day.

7
00:00:13,459 --> 00:00:16,040
It is a Great Day for everyone born

8
00:00:16,090 --> 00:00:17,995
in the Year of the Monkey.

9
00:00:18,045 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,488
Playful and clever

11
00:00:21,538 --> 00:00:24,394
the Monkey symbolizes intelligence

12
00:00:24,444 --> 00:00:26,873
curiosity, and joyful energy.

13
00:00:26,923 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,753
People born in the Year of the Monkey

15
00:00:32,803 --> 00:00:35,102
are clever, curious, and witty.

16
00:00:35,152 --> 00:00:37,374
Monkeys are quick learners who

17
00:00:37,424 --> 00:00:39,950
enjoy solving problems creatively.

18
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,806
Born in the Year of the Monkey?

19
00:00:41,856 --> 00:00:43,363
Your luck is strong today.

20
00:00:43,413 --> 00:00:45,399
Stay playful, clever and cheerful.

21
00:00:45,449 --> 00:00:47,435
Say yes to good chances, and share

22
00:00:47,485 --> 00:00:49,950
your happiness with the people around you.

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,394
Love and family today:

24
00:00:52,444 --> 00:00:56,506
A good day to reach out, share a meal

25
00:00:56,556 --> 00:00:59,950
and show your affection openly.

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,969
Work today: Customers

27
00:01:02,019 --> 00:01:05,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

28
00:01:05,288 --> 00:01:08,315
A good day to present your ideas

29
00:01:08,365 --> 00:01:09,950
and close a deal.

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,805
Money today: Good for fair deals

31
00:01:13,855 --> 00:01:17,179
and collecting what is owed.

32
00:01:17,229 --> 00:01:19,950
Keep your records neat.

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,718
Your lucky color today is blue.

34
00:01:22,768 --> 00:01:26,468
Wear something blue, or carry a small blue

35
00:01:26,518 --> 00:01:29,950
item with you, to keep good luck close.

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,689
Your best time today

37
00:01:31,739 --> 00:01:34,298
is from 7 to 9 in the morning.

38
00:01:34,348 --> 00:01:36,993
It is a good time for important

39
00:01:37,043 --> 00:01:39,950
plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

40
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:41,431
Your lucky direction

41
00:01:41,481 --> 00:01:43,135
today is the southeast.

42
00:01:43,185 --> 00:01:45,209
When you start something new

43
00:01:45,259 --> 00:01:47,876
face the southeast, and walk forward

44
00:01:47,926 --> 00:01:49,950
with a calm and happy heart.

45
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,350
For lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449.

46
00:01:53,400 --> 00:01:56,450
Comment your lucky number below

47
00:01:56,500 --> 00:01:59,950
and let's say it again: 9, 90, 449!

48
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,252
The Monkey's lucky days of the week

49
00:02:03,302 --> 00:02:05,610
are Wednesday and Friday.

50
00:02:05,660 --> 00:02:08,158
Keep them in mind for plans

51
00:02:08,208 --> 00:02:09,950
that matter to you.

52
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,695
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year

53
00:02:12,745 --> 00:02:14,787
of the Monkey can be mischievous

54
00:02:14,837 --> 00:02:17,009
or struggle to focus on one thing.

55
00:02:17,059 --> 00:02:18,316
Today, stay balanced

56
00:02:18,366 --> 00:02:19,950
and listen to your heart.

57
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,080
Make a small wish today

58
00:02:22,130 --> 00:02:25,135
and take one kind step toward it.

59
00:02:25,185 --> 00:02:27,172
A peaceful heart helps

60
00:02:27,222 --> 00:02:29,950
good luck find its way to you.

61
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,470
Before the day ends

62
00:02:31,520 --> 00:02:34,430
take a quiet moment to say thank you.

63
00:02:34,480 --> 00:02:36,750
Thank the people who help you

64
00:02:36,800 --> 00:02:39,950
and thank yourself for trying your best.

65
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,936
Let's remember today's luck.

66
00:02:41,986 --> 00:02:43,212
Lucky color: blue.

67
00:02:43,262 --> 00:02:45,907
Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning.

68
00:02:45,957 --> 00:02:47,822

ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។ ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

"The Golden Monkey and the River of Stars" (ស្វាមាស និងទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden monkey and Pich the butterfly must bring the stars back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Pich once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Monkey picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Pich and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Pich the butterfly (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Pich, a tiny, lifelike magical butterfly with bright, expressive eyes and shimmering pink-and-gold wings that leave a trail of glitter, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

នៅថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក ស្វាមាសតូចមួយ និងមេអំបៅតូចឈ្មោះពេជ្រ ត្រូវស្វែងរករតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី ដើម្បីធ្វើឲ្យទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ មុនពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់។ ជារឿងនិទានគំនូរជីវចលសម្រាប់គ្រួសារទាំងមូល ជាមួយពណ៌សំណាង ទិសសំណាង ពេលល្អ និងលេខសំណាងពិតប្រាកដ សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវកថ្ងៃនេះ។

ជំពូក៖
0:00 កាលពីព្រេងនាយ
1:20 ពណ៌សំណាង
1:40 ផ្លូវសំណាង
2:30 ទ្វារថ្ម
3:40 ពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់
4:30 សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ

ធាតុថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ឈើ (យ៉ាង) 🌿

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the monkey is the saved Flow character @Monkey. She greets the viewer with a soft laugh and shining eyes, warm and close, as if she has been waiting all day to share this moment. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical.
English voice: Audio: Chanda's voice, identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices. Chanda is on camera and speaks to the viewer with natural lip movements that match her words. She says: "Can one little golden monkey bring the stars back before the full moon rises? ## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden monkey bring the stars back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the stars in the stream began to fade. Soon the water was dark. Without its light, no one could find the way to the festival.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden monkey. Today was a Monkey day, and the monkey felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down. "Little Monkey," she said, "only you can bring the stars back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Pich smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To make the river of stars shine again, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the monkey, and it set off on its adventure, with Pich right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are clever, curious, and witty. Monkeys are quick learners who enjoy solving problems creatively.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was blue, the monkey's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The monkey tied a blue ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Pich, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southeast, the monkey's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The monkey stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Monkey can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Pich came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the monkey climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Pich. For the Monkey today, that is from 7 to 9 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the monkey rang the golden bells 9 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The monkey climbed 90 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a golden star stone, bright and warm. It was the heart of the river of stars!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the star stone, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Friday shone brightest, the Monkey's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the star stone carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Pich right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The monkey dropped the star stone into the stream. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, sparkle! Thousands of stars lit up the water!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The river of stars shone again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The monkey felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Monkey, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: blue. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Pich fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

តើស្វាមាសតូចមួយ អាចនាំផ្កាយត្រឡប់មកវិញ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ? កាលពីព្រេងនាយ នៅក្បែរប្រាសាទបុរាណមួយ ក្នុងប្រទេសកម្ពុជា រឿងរបស់យើងចាប់ផ្ដើម។

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ផ្កាយនៅក្នុងអូរ ចាប់ផ្ដើមស្រអាប់។ មិនយូរប៉ុន្មាន ទឹកក៏ងងឹត។ បើគ្មានពន្លឺរបស់វា គ្មាននរណាអាចរកផ្លូវទៅពិធីបុណ្យបានទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានស្វាមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក ហើយស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

មេអំបៅតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះពេជ្រ ហើរចុះមក។ ពេជ្រនិយាយថា "ស្វាតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំផ្កាយត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ស្វា ចូលចិត្តលេងសើច និងឆ្លាតវៃ។ ឆ្នាំវកតំណាងឲ្យបញ្ញា ការចង់ដឹងចង់ឃើញ និងភាពរីករាយ។ ពេជ្រញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីធ្វើឲ្យទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ស្វាឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនកៅសិប កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញថ្មរូបផ្កាយពណ៌មាស ភ្លឺ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញថ្មផ្កាយ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំវក។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ថ្មផ្កាយយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ស្វាទម្លាក់ថ្មផ្កាយ ចូលក្នុងអូរ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក ភ្លឹបភ្លែត! ផ្កាយរាប់ពាន់ បំភ្លឺផ្ទៃទឹក!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខកៅសិប និងលេខបួនរយសែសិបប្រាំបួន។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

70
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,822
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ស្វាវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

71
00:02:52,872 --> 00:02:56,652
ចំនួន 9 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

72
00:02:56,702 --> 00:02:57,929
ទីង!

73
00:02:57,979 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

74
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,254
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

75
00:03:03,304 --> 00:03:05,932
ស្វាឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 90 កាំ

76
00:03:05,982 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

77
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,728
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

78
00:03:11,778 --> 00:03:15,061
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញថ្មរូបផ្កាយពណ៌មាស

79
00:03:15,111 --> 00:03:16,839
ភ្លឺ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។

80
00:03:16,889 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ!

81
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,085
នៅជុំវិញថ្មផ្កាយ

82
00:03:21,135 --> 00:03:23,638
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

83
00:03:23,688 --> 00:03:25,978
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

84
00:03:26,028 --> 00:03:28,106
ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

85
00:03:28,156 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំវក។

86
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,600
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

87
00:03:31,650 --> 00:03:35,484
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ថ្មផ្កាយយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

88
00:03:35,534 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានពេជ្រ នៅក្បែរ។

89
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

90
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

91
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

92
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,886
ស្វាទម្លាក់ថ្មផ្កាយ ចូលក្នុងអូរ។

93
00:03:52,936 --> 00:03:55,455
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

94
00:03:55,505 --> 00:03:57,381
បន្ទាប់មក ភ្លឹបភ្លែត!

95
00:03:57,431 --> 00:03:59,950
ផ្កាយរាប់ពាន់ បំភ្លឺផ្ទៃទឹក!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,037
ទន្លេនៃផ្កាយ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។

97
00:04:02,087 --> 00:04:03,254
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,304 --> 00:04:06,646
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,696 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,121
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,171 --> 00:04:14,991
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:15,041 --> 00:04:17,836
ស្វាមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,886 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,863
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,913 --> 00:04:26,559
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,609 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:32,990
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

109
00:04:33,040 --> 00:04:35,470
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

110
00:04:35,520 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

111
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 90 និងលេខ 449។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,061
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

113
00:04:44,111 --> 00:04:47,506
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងពេជ្រ ក៏លង់លក់

114
00:04:47,556 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

115
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

116
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

117
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

118
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

119
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-06-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,247
Can one little golden monkey bring

2
00:00:02,297 --> 00:00:05,085
the stars back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,135 --> 00:00:07,923
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,973 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,118
But this morning

6
00:00:11,168 --> 00:00:13,892
the stars in the stream began to fade.

7
00:00:13,942 --> 00:00:15,643
Soon the water was dark.

8
00:00:15,693 --> 00:00:16,884
Without its light

9
00:00:16,934 --> 00:00:19,950
no one could find the way to the festival.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,711
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,761 --> 00:00:23,189
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,239 --> 00:00:25,725
and out came a little golden monkey.

13
00:00:25,775 --> 00:00:27,274
Today was a Monkey day

14
00:00:27,324 --> 00:00:29,950
and the monkey felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,039
A butterfly named Pich fluttered down.

16
00:00:33,089 --> 00:00:35,072
"Little Monkey," she said

17
00:00:35,122 --> 00:00:37,917
"only you can bring the stars back.

18
00:00:37,967 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,274
Playful and clever

20
00:00:41,324 --> 00:00:43,774
the Monkey symbolizes intelligence

21
00:00:43,824 --> 00:00:45,906
curiosity, and joyful energy.

22
00:00:45,956 --> 00:00:46,788
Pich smiled.

23
00:00:46,838 --> 00:00:48,259
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,309 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,744
To make the river of stars shine again

26
00:00:52,794 --> 00:00:54,803
the little hero needed three

27
00:00:54,853 --> 00:00:57,156
lucky treasures: its lucky color

28
00:00:57,206 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,090
"Let's go!" said the monkey

30
00:01:03,140 --> 00:01:06,694
and it set off on its adventure

31
00:01:06,744 --> 00:01:09,950
with Pich right by its side.

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,753
People born in the Year of the Monkey

33
00:01:12,803 --> 00:01:15,102
are clever, curious, and witty.

34
00:01:15,152 --> 00:01:17,374
Monkeys are quick learners who

35
00:01:17,424 --> 00:01:19,950
enjoy solving problems creatively.

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,206
The first treasure was waiting

37
00:01:22,256 --> 00:01:24,837
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

38
00:01:24,887 --> 00:01:26,717
Only one shone brightest.

39
00:01:26,767 --> 00:01:28,597
It was blue, the monkey's

40
00:01:28,647 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

41
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,880
The monkey tied a blue

42
00:01:31,930 --> 00:01:33,897
ribbon around its neck.

43
00:01:33,947 --> 00:01:36,880
"Wear your lucky color," said Pich

44
00:01:36,930 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

45
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,410
Next, they came to a crossroads

46
00:01:42,460 --> 00:01:43,680
with many paths.

47
00:01:43,730 --> 00:01:45,506
One path began to glow.

48
00:01:45,556 --> 00:01:47,648
It pointed to the southeast

49
00:01:47,698 --> 00:01:49,950
the monkey's lucky direction.

50
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,058
But the path climbed a steep stone hill

51
00:01:52,108 --> 00:01:53,518
with a strong wind blowing.

52
00:01:53,568 --> 00:01:54,545
The monkey stopped.

53
00:01:54,595 --> 00:01:56,382
Sometimes, people born in the Year

54
00:01:56,432 --> 00:01:58,112
of the Monkey can be mischievous

55
00:01:58,162 --> 00:01:59,950
or struggle to focus on one thing.

56
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,521
Pich came close and smiled.

57
00:02:02,571 --> 00:02:05,093
"Breathe slowly," she said.

58
00:02:05,143 --> 00:02:06,807
"Trust your heart.

59
00:02:06,857 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

60
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,236
Step by step, the monkey climbed the hill.

61
00:02:14,286 --> 00:02:16,277
The wind grew softer

62
00:02:16,327 --> 00:02:19,950
and at the top, it laughed with joy.

63
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

64
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

68
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

69
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

70
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,525
"The door opens only

71
00:02:41,575 --> 00:02:43,887
at your best time," said Pich.

72
00:02:43,937 --> 00:02:45,462
For the Monkey today

73
00:02:45,512 --> 00:02:48,218
that is from 7 to 9 in the morning.

74
00:02:48,268 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

75
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,488
When the time came

76
00:02:51,538 --> 00:02:54,907
the monkey rang the golden bells 9 times

77
00:02:54,957 --> 00:02:56,873
its first lucky number.

78
00:02:56,923 --> 00:02:57,899
Ding!

79
00:02:57,949 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

80
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,078
The door opened

81
00:03:01,128 --> 00:03:03,559
and a stairway of light appeared.

82
00:03:03,609 --> 00:03:06,191
The monkey climbed 90 glowing steps

83
00:03:06,241 --> 00:03:07,920
its second lucky number

84
00:03:07,970 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

85
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:13,103
At the top of the tower, they found

86
00:03:13,153 --> 00:03:16,436
a golden star stone, bright and warm.

87
00:03:16,486 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the river of stars!

88
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,929
Around the star stone, seven little lights

89
00:03:22,979 --> 00:03:25,695
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

90
00:03:25,745 --> 00:03:28,248
Wednesday and Friday shone brightest

91
00:03:28,298 --> 00:03:29,950
the Monkey's lucky days.

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,913
Time was running out.

93
00:03:31,963 --> 00:03:34,903
Holding the star stone carefully

94
00:03:34,953 --> 00:03:37,520
the little hero hurried home

95
00:03:37,570 --> 00:03:39,950
with Pich right beside it.

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,027
The monkey dropped the star

101
00:03:52,077 --> 00:03:53,719
stone into the stream.

102
00:03:53,769 --> 00:03:56,104
For a moment, nothing happened.

103
00:03:56,154 --> 00:03:57,181
Then, sparkle!

104
00:03:57,231 --> 00:03:59,950
Thousands of stars lit up the water!

105
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,281
The river of stars shone again.

106
00:04:02,331 --> 00:04:04,236
Lanterns rose into the sky

107
00:04:04,286 --> 00:04:06,717
and the full-moon festival began.

108
00:04:06,767 --> 00:04:07,920
Everyone cheered

109
00:04:07,970 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

110
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,646
The full moon shone down warmly

111
00:04:12,696 --> 00:04:14,646
as if to say thank you.

112
00:04:14,696 --> 00:04:17,689
The monkey felt peaceful and proud.

113
00:04:17,739 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

114
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,208
If you were born in the Year

115
00:04:22,258 --> 00:04:25,192
of the Monkey, today is your day too.

116
00:04:25,242 --> 00:04:27,773
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

117
00:04:27,823 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

118
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,936
Let's remember today's luck.

119
00:04:31,986 --> 00:04:33,212
Lucky color: blue.

120
00:04:33,262 --> 00:04:35,907
Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning.

121
00:04:35,957 --> 00:04:37,822
Lucky direction: southeast.

122
00:04:37,872 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449.

123
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

124
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Pich fell

125
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

126
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

127
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

128
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

129
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

130
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

131
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Tiger (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Tiger, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Tiger clashes with the Monkey day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Tiger as ingredients (the Tiger is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the tiger hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little golden firefly keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Tiger? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខាល រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Tiger, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខាល ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Tiger, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខាល នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Tiger? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខាលទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ 5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Tiger.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំខាល។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Scene 7 – Brighter days ahead (1:00–1:10)

@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Tiger.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 6 November, is a Monkey day, opposite the Tiger in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be impulsive or impatient with slower-moving people. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Monkey) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rat and Dragon), the sign to take it easy (Tiger) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Monkey
2:40 Good Day: Rat
3:30 Good Day: Dragon
4:20 Take it easy: Tiger
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
★ 2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Tiger? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down. Share your luck.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Monkey day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Monkey, Rat and Dragon are lucky today, and the Tiger should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Friday, 6 November: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Monkey! Let's see what this lucky day brings you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Playful and clever, the Monkey symbolizes intelligence, curiosity, and joyful energy. Keep that energy close all day.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Monkey are often described as clever, curious, and witty. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
This is your day. It's a Yang Wood day, a day of growth.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly. Share your luck.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Today's lucky color is blue. A blue scarf, bag or bracelet keeps it close. Let it remind you to smile and stay confident.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Lucky direction: the southeast. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the morning. Use that time for calls and big decisions.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 9, 90, and 449. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Monkey's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them for plans that matter. Keep a calm heart.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Monkey people can be mischievous or struggle to focus on one thing. Stay balanced today. Small choices bring big luck.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Next, the Year of the Rat: a Good Day! The Rat shares a lucky triangle with the Monkey. Share your luck.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
A good day to meet people and start something nice. Love: Spend a little quality time with someone close. A Yang Wood day!

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)

ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ខៀវ។

41
00:01:52,212 --> 00:01:56,145
កន្សែង កាបូប ឬខ្សែដៃពណ៌ខៀវ នាំសំណាងមកជិតអ្នក។

42
00:01:56,195 --> 00:01:59,950
សូមឲ្យវារំលឹកអ្នក ឲ្យញញឹម និងមានទំនុកចិត្ត។

43
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,932
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

44
00:02:01,982 --> 00:02:05,175
ពេលល្អបំផុត៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

45
00:02:05,225 --> 00:02:08,058
សូមប្រើពេលនោះ សម្រាប់ការទូរស័ព្ទ

46
00:02:08,108 --> 00:02:09,950
និងការសម្រេចចិត្តធំៗ។

47
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,899
លេខសំណាង៖ 9 90 និង 449។

48
00:02:12,949 --> 00:02:17,258
សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់

49
00:02:17,308 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

50
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:24,839
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំវក គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ។

51
00:02:24,889 --> 00:02:27,839
សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។

52
00:02:27,889 --> 00:02:29,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

53
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,450
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក

54
00:02:32,500 --> 00:02:36,339
ជួនកាលលេងកំប្លែងពេក ឬពិបាកផ្តោតលើរឿងតែមួយ។

55
00:02:36,389 --> 00:02:37,913
សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។

56
00:02:37,963 --> 00:02:39,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

57
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:42,905
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

58
00:02:42,955 --> 00:02:48,018
ឆ្នាំជូត ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំវក។

59
00:02:48,068 --> 00:02:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

60
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,405
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

61
00:02:52,455 --> 00:02:54,223
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

62
00:02:54,273 --> 00:02:55,314
ស្នេហា៖

63
00:02:55,364 --> 00:02:58,859
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

64
00:02:58,909 --> 00:02:59,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

65
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:05,520
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ខៀវ ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល

66
00:03:05,570 --> 00:03:07,545
ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

67
00:03:07,595 --> 00:03:09,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

68
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,325
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។

69
00:03:12,375 --> 00:03:15,200
លេខសំណាង៖ 3 29 និង 234។

70
00:03:15,250 --> 00:03:19,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

71
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,450
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំជូត ថ្ងៃនេះ

72
00:03:22,500 --> 00:03:24,950
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក។

73
00:03:25,000 --> 00:03:28,102
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

74
00:03:28,152 --> 00:03:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

75
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:33,083
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

76
00:03:33,133 --> 00:03:38,504
ឆ្នាំរោង ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំវក។

77
00:03:38,554 --> 00:03:39,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

78
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:42,258
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

79
00:03:42,308 --> 00:03:43,967
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

80
00:03:44,017 --> 00:03:44,993
ស្នេហា៖

81
00:03:45,043 --> 00:03:48,326
សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។

82
00:03:48,376 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

83
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,185
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ប្រាក់

84
00:03:52,235 --> 00:03:57,362
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

85
00:03:57,412 --> 00:03:59,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

86
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,325
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។

87
00:04:02,375 --> 00:04:05,200
លេខសំណាង៖ 6 67 និង 719។

88
00:04:05,250 --> 00:04:09,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

89
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,594
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ

90
00:04:12,644 --> 00:04:15,237
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំវក។

91
00:04:15,287 --> 00:04:18,571
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

92
00:04:18,621 --> 00:04:19,950
ថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ!

93
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,711
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 6 ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំវក

94
00:04:23,761 --> 00:04:26,556
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំខាល ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

95
00:04:26,606 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

96
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

97
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

98
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

99
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

100
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,176
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលប្រញាប់ប្រញាល់

101
00:04:43,226 --> 00:04:46,402
ឬមិនសូវអត់ធ្មត់ចំពោះអ្នកដែលធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺត។

102
00:04:46,452 --> 00:04:48,579
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

103
00:04:48,629 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

104
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

105
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

106
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

107
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:01,897
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

108
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

109
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

110
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950
សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

111
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

112
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

113
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

114
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,564
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

115
00:05:22,614 --> 00:05:24,495
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

116
00:05:24,545 --> 00:05:28,018
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ល្ងាច។

117
00:05:28,068 --> 00:05:29,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

118
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:34,338
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

119
00:05:34,388 --> 00:05:36,379
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។

120
00:05:36,429 --> 00:05:39,950
ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

121
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:42,258
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

122
00:05:42,308 --> 00:05:44,565
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ផ្កាឈូក។

123
00:05:44,615 --> 00:05:47,862
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 យប់។

124
00:05:47,912 --> 00:05:49,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

125
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:53,363
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

126
00:05:53,413 --> 00:05:57,013
លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

127
00:05:57,063 --> 00:05:58,601
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

128
00:05:58,651 --> 00:05:59,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

129
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,647
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

130
00:06:02,697 --> 00:06:04,557
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

131
00:06:04,607 --> 00:06:08,040
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ល្ងាច។

132
00:06:08,090 --> 00:06:09,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

133
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:14,166
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

134
00:06:14,216 --> 00:06:16,126
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងលិច។

135
00:06:16,176 --> 00:06:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃធាតុឈើ ជាថ្ងៃនៃការលូតលាស់។

136
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,392
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

137
00:06:22,442 --> 00:06:24,369
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

138
00:06:24,419 --> 00:06:27,741
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 យប់។

139
00:06:27,791 --> 00:06:29,950
សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

140
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:30,910
ស្នេហា៖

141
00:06:30,960 --> 00:06:34,030
សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ

142
00:06:34,080 --> 00:06:36,590
ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។

143
00:06:36,640 --> 00:06:38,190
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

144
00:06:38,240 --> 00:06:39,950
ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

145
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,364
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

146
00:06:42,414 --> 00:06:44,548
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

147
00:06:44,598 --> 00:06:47,996
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 យប់។

148
00:06:48,046 --> 00:06:49,950
សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

149
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:54,728
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

## Scene 1 – Monkey day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She greets the viewer with a soft laugh and shining eyes, warm and close, as if she has been waiting all day to share this moment. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it chases its guardian across the rooftops in a game of tag, both leaping and spinning in slow-motion acrobatics against the full moon, as today's Wood magic swirls around them: fresh green leaves, glowing jade vines and young bamboo shoots that sprout and spiral through the air. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and mist swirls away in glowing rings. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey), @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) and @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, long ribbons of golden light unfurl from the temple towers and wave above them. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Tiger, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Tiger (the Tiger guardian with their tiger) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it rests quietly as a few fireflies keep watch around it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The tiger is the saved Flow character @Tiger.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Hundreds of sky lanterns rise from behind the temple and form a great glowing circle around the full moon, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
